Kíli the Resourceful (trigger already on the stack): Respond to Kíli's draw trigger with Wasp to stop the draw. An ability on the stack exists independently of its source; blanking Kíli does not remove or counter it. Only Tidebinder stops the draw. Wasp Kíli before their main phase instead.
Thorin, Mountain-king: Wasp Thorin in response to his ETB to stop the attach and the damage. Same rule: the ETB is already on the stack and resolves regardless. Wasp on Thorin only takes Trample from a body that already did its job. If Thorin targets himself, kill him in response (Sheriff/Triumph) or counter the ETB with Tidebinder.
An attacker already declared: Wasp taps it so it stops attacking. Tapping an attacking creature does not remove it from combat. The blank still strips double strike and trample if the Axe was attached earlier, which is real, but the damage still comes.
Raubahn once equipped: Pay the ward and blank him. 'Ward, Pay life equal to Raubahn's power' scales with his equipped power; at 6+ power you are paying a quarter of your life for a tap. Either skip him or have Tidebinder counter the ward trigger (no life paid, Raubahn blanked).
Wasp as a permanent answer: Once Wasped, the creature is dealt with. The loss lasts only 'for as long as The Wondrous Wasp remains on the battlefield'. Wasp is a 2/1 that dies to Chainsaw, Thorin's damage trigger or a Kaito ninjutsu return, and everything comes back the moment it leaves.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • Thorin's ETB, cheap version: if he targets himself (the common line, to pick up Boots for haste) and does not yet wear the Blade, Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph on Thorin in response kills him and the whole trigger does nothing (illegal creature target, CR 608.2b); if he targets a mana value 2 or less carrier without the Blade, Requiting Hex does the same for {B}. Tidebinder is for when the target already wears Dragonfire Blade or you have no removal.
  • Tidebinder counters Thorin's ETB, then Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph on Thorin in the same turn cycle; a 3/4 with no Trample is not worth protecting and the Equipment never moved.
  • Tidebinder counters a Dragonfire Blade re-equip (their free Kíli equip is spent for the turn), the Blade goes blank and cannot be equipped again while Tidebinder lives, then Requiting Hex / Shoot the Sheriff / Floodpits Drowner the formerly hexproof creature before Tidebinder can die. Kill order matters: the Blade wakes up the moment Tidebinder leaves.
  • Kaito -2 (tap plus two stun counters) is the removal that ignores Dragonfire Blade: Kaito is blue-black, so his ability is not monocolored. Save it for the Blade carrier and use Wasp/Drowner on the other attacker. Casting Kaito on 4 is usually better than holding Tidebinder for Thorin when Kaito is in hand.
  • Enduring Curiosity has flash: on your T4 with Curiosity in hand, passing with four open is free. Thorin shows up, Tidebinder his ETB; he does not, flash Curiosity at their end step.
  • Wasp at their beginning of combat on the Axe carrier that is not wearing the Blade; they cannot refresh Axe's double strike by re-equipping it to the same creature (CR 701.3b), so the swing stays single-strike until they move Equipment around or Wasp dies.
  • Raubahn already equipped: Wasp, Shoot the Sheriff or Requiting Hex targets him, his ward trigger goes on the stack, Tidebinder counters the ward trigger. No life paid, Raubahn loses all abilities, the original spell resolves. Illegal only if he wears Dragonfire Blade.
  • Wasp on Kíli at their upkeep when Requiting Hex is not in hand, then Hex the blank Kíli when you draw it; both cards cost 1-2 mana and the Wasp keeps flying for Enduring Curiosity draws.
  • Attack with Wasp in the air (no fliers or reach in their 75), ninjutsu Kaito ({1}{U}{B}) off the unblocked Wasp; Wasp returns to hand, its blank ends, and you flash it back later for a fresh tap-and-blank.
  • Spell Snare counters every mana value 2 spell in their 75: Dáin's Company, Giott, Kíli, Dwalin, Raubahn, The Queen of Dale, Shattered Acolyte, Chainsaw, Rest in Peace and Bofur's Adventure half. Cavern of Souls (naming Dwarf) protects only the Dwarf creature spells among these; Raubahn and Queen are Humans, Chainsaw and RIP are not creatures.
  • Post-board Flashfreeze ({1}{U}, 'Counter target red or green spell') counters Thorin, Chainsaw, Dáin's, Giott, Dwalin, Raubahn and Mauler unless Cavern mana was used; it is the cheaper Thorin answer when he is not Cavern-cast. It never hits Kíli, Fíli, Queen, Acolyte, Erode, Bofur or RIP (white).
  • Annul (side, 1) is the hard counter for Dragonfire Blade, Leyline Axe, Tentacles, Boots, Chainsaw and Rest in Peace on cast; Cavern does not protect noncreature spells. We Say Thee Nay! ({2}, or {4} with teamwork) and Spell Pierce ({2}) are taxes that only work when they are tapped low.
  • Day of Black Sun X=2 (side, 2) kills every Dwarves creature except Thorin and Fíli, ignores Blade hexproof and Bofur's indestructible (they lose all abilities first), but also kills our Beavers, Siren, Drowner and Wasp; Tidebinder (MV 3), Curiosity (MV 4) and Kaito survive. Cast it before deploying those, not after.
  • Strategic Betrayal (side, 1): 'Target opponent exiles a creature they control' means THEY choose, so it only answers the Blade carrier when it is their only creature.
  • Post-board with 3 Tidebinder: use the first on the earliest Dragonfire Blade re-equip or a Kíli trigger you cannot Hex, hold the second for Thorin's ETB, and fire the third at Chainsaw's ETB when it aims at a Tidebinder, a Wasp holding a key blank, or your ninjutsu flier.
Hold or fire
Single main-deck Tidebinder. First, know your windows: on the play your first Tidebinder window is their T3 (hold your own T3 open); on the draw it is their T4, the same turn Thorin arrives, and you have exactly four lands. The first Dragonfire Blade equip is their T2 and is never catchable; the Blade targets that exist are re-equips and second Blades. Default plan: hold Tidebinder for Thorin's ETB, with two big exceptions. (1) If Kaito is in hand on your T4 and no Thorin has been revealed (Dáin's dig, discard), cast Kaito; Kaito -2 handles the Blade carrier and a held Tidebinder that never fires loses you the game to a 5/4 double striker. Deploy Tidebinder at an end step as a 3/2 by turn 5-6 rather than letting it rot. (2) If Enduring Curiosity is in hand, holding four mana is free: Tidebinder if Thorin shows, Curiosity at their end step if not. Fire Tidebinder early only on a Dragonfire Blade re-equip onto their main threat or a Kíli draw trigger you cannot answer with Requiting Hex. Never Tidebinder a Kíli or Dwalin trigger while Hex is in hand; Hex is permanent and costs {B}. Also remember the cheap Thorin answer: if his ETB targets a creature you can legally kill (Thorin himself before the Blade lands, or an MV 2 or less carrier without the Blade), kill it in response and keep Tidebinder. Wasp: fire early. If Requiting Hex is not in hand, Wasp Kíli at their upkeep. Otherwise hold Wasp for their beginning of combat and tap the Axe or Tentacles carrier that is not wearing the Blade; that blank sticks until Wasp dies or they move Equipment. On your turn, Wasp can clear a blocker for the ninjutsu Wasp attack. Post-board with 3 Tidebinder, fire the first at anything from priority 1-4, keep one for Thorin.
How many to run
Tidebinder: bring both sideboard copies in (3 total, up from 1). The targets that justify it: Thorin's ETB when its target is Blade-protected (nothing else in our 75 stops that), Dragonfire Blade re-equips (the only way to switch off a resolved Blade), Chainsaw's ETB if they bring it in, plus Kíli/Dwalin/Raubahn triggers as tempo when Hex is not in hand. It does not care about ward or hexproof. Cut 2 Wan Shi Tong, Librarian: an X spell is too slow against T1 pressure and its 'whenever an opponent searches' trigger never fires here (Erode lets the destroyed creature's controller search, which is us). Keep all 23 lands: the whole plan is holding three open on their T3/T4 while still casting 4-mana Kaito and Curiosity. Keep Spell Snare: it counters ten different MV 2 spells in their 75 and only Cavern beats it on Dwarf creatures. Also consider 2 Flashfreeze (hits Thorin, Chainsaw and every red Dwarf) and 2 Day of Black Sun (X=2 sweeps everything but Thorin/Fíli through hexproof), cutting We Say Thee Nay! (their spells cost 1-2, two Caverns blank it on Dwarves) and 1-2 Bitter Triumph if space is tight. Wasp: keep 2, do not add. The Blade carrier is immune, Raubahn taxes life, Kíli is better answered by Hex, and a 2/1 dies to Chainsaw and every double striker; its value is the beginning-of-combat tap on the non-Blade attacker plus Curiosity draws, which two copies already provide.
Review trail: confidence high, 9 disputed, 25 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Kept: ward never taxes Tidebinder (against the brief's 'Ward taxes ... Tidebinder's trigger'). Ward (CR 702.21b) triggers when the permanent 'becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls'; Tidebinder targets an activated or triggered ability on the stack, never the permanent. Raubahn's 'Ward, Pay life equal to Raubahn's power' and Lavaspur Boots' 'ward {1}' tax Wasp, Drowner and our removal, not Tidebinder. Reviewer 2 independently verified this.
  • Kept: Doc Ock's Tentacles' 'Whenever a creature you control with mana value 5 or greater enters' never fires here (ability_tags hint rejected). Highest creature mana value in the 75 is 4 (Thorin {3}{R}, Fíli {3}{W}); tokens are MV 0. The Tidebinder hook on Tentacles is 'Equip {5}'.
  • Kept: equip is an activated ability (CR 702.6a) on Dragonfire Blade, Lavaspur Boots, Doc Ock's Tentacles, Basilisk Collar and Chainsaw even though the ability_tags regex only caught Axe and Collar; countering any of them blanks that Equipment while Tidebinder remains.
  • Rejected Reviewer 2's claim that Bofur's adventure 'saves a Blade from Annul': the adventure reads 'Target artifact or creature you control gains hexproof and indestructible until end of turn' and targets a permanent on the battlefield; Annul counters the Blade as a spell on the stack, where Bofur cannot reach it. Bofur does fizzle Wasp, Drowner and mono-black removal aimed at the creature, which is what the trap entry now says.
  • Rejected Reviewer 2's suggested Hidden Lair / land cut framing only in that the draft's own land-cut suggestion was wrong; accepted the correction (keep 23 lands, cut Wan Shi Tong).
  • Partially rejected Reviewer 2's 'permanent removal of printed abilities' wording complaint as a rules error in the Wasp section: the draft's sentence was imprecise, not false in context, but it has been reworded to 'for as long as Wasp stays on the battlefield' per the oracle text.
  • Rejected 'Strategic Betrayal is a non-targeting out to a Blade carrier' as stated in the draft: the oracle text 'Target opponent exiles a creature they control' lets the opponent choose, so it answers the Blade carrier only when it is their sole creature. Corrected in combined_lines.
  • Hone counters: all three reviewers asked for timestamp treatment; accepted with an explicit uncertainty flag. The reminder text 'Each hone counter on an Equipment grants +1/+0 to equipped creature' reads as an ability of the Equipment (so earlier counters go silent on a blanked Equipment and later ones work, matching Tidebinder ruling 2), but the CR entry for hone counters was not in our data and should be confirmed against the set's release notes before the page treats it as settled.
  • Verdict downgrade to 'good' accepted from Reviewer 2 on strategic grounds (window timing on the draw, Kaito tradeoff, Requiting Hex overlap), not on any rules error; the target analysis is unchanged.
Changes after review
  • Verdict for Tidebinder changed from premium to good; summary rewritten to name the two premium jobs and the reasons it is not premium (T2 first Blade equip, Kaito on 4, Hex overlap, fragile blank).
  • Thorin entry: added the in-response kill of the ETB's creature target (Sheriff/Triumph on Thorin, Hex on an MV<=2 non-Blade carrier) with CR 608.2b; removed 'Nothing else in our 75 stops it'; fixed on-the-draw timing.
  • Dragonfire Blade entry: retitled as re-equip window; after_effect corrected so a blank Blade cannot be equipped again (no Equip ability exists, Kíli's {0} needs an equip ability) and only Thorin/Raubahn can move it.
  • Kíli entries (Tidebinder and Wasp): Storied treated correctly (blank Kíli cannot grant a not-yet-earned story; an earned story persists); Wasp on Kíli demoted below the equipped-attacker tap and made conditional on no Requiting Hex.
  • how_they_win: Kíli draw is once per turn; story timing (Kíli plus two artifacts/legendaries, Kíli counts); 'only real removal' now 'only main-deck removal' with Erode/Chainsaw post-board; added the Thorin-self-target Boots haste line and the Tentacles equip cost onto Mauler.
  • Chainsaw entry: moved up to priority 4; added the second trigger ('Whenever one or more creatures die, put a rev counter') as a later blank window; dropped 'save Curiosity' as a reason (it returns as an enchantment) in favour of saving Tidebinder/Wasp/ninjutsu flier.
  • Added Lavaspur Boots equip {1} (priority 10) and Basilisk Collar equip {2} (priority 11) as Tidebinder targets with honest low value.
  • Added the Raubahn ward-trigger line (Wasp/Sheriff/Hex targets him, Tidebinder counters the ward trigger, no life paid, Raubahn blanked) to his Tidebinder entry, Wasp entry, traps and combined_lines.
  • Removed the five-mana Boots ward combo from combined_lines; added a trap saying to pay the {1} instead.
  • Hone counters: both mentions now use timestamp logic (earlier counters silent on a blanked Equipment, later ones work) with an explicit 'confirm against release notes' flag; Dwalin after_effect says earlier counters keep working only if the Equipment itself is not blanked.
  • Leyline Axe trap: 'static that resolves before priority' replaced with 'static ability enabling a pre-game action (CR 103.6); nothing uses the stack'.
  • Cavern of Souls trap: reason corrected (two mana abilities plus an as-enters choice that never uses the stack).
  • Shattered Acolyte: noted it can destroy Enduring Curiosity in creature form (it is always an enchantment) and two Acolytes remove it for good; Hex answers Acolyte, Sheriff cannot.
  • Rest in Peace trap: added that RIP stops Enduring Curiosity from returning (exiled, never dies); Annul or race it.
  • Queen of Dale: Hex plus Tidebinder is four mana, T4 not T5.
  • Tentacles entry: Thorin/Raubahn are windows to blank Thorin/Raubahn, not Tentacles; paid equip is {3} onto Mauler.
  • Spell Snare line: added Shattered Acolyte, Rest in Peace and Bofur's Adventure half; Cavern protects only Dwarf creature spells (Raubahn and Queen are Humans).
  • We Say Thee Nay! and Spell Pierce labelled as taxes ({2}/{4}, {2}); Annul is the hard counter.
  • Strategic Betrayal corrected: opponent chooses the creature, so it only works when the Blade carrier is their sole creature.
  • Wasp section: equipped non-Blade attacker moved to priority 1 with the CR 701.3b note that re-equipping the same Equipment to the same creature does not refresh the timestamp; added Dwarven Mauler pre-equip blank (priority 6); 'permanent removal' reworded to 'while Wasp stays on the battlefield'; added a trap about Wasp not being permanent.
  • Added combined_lines for the cheap Thorin answer, the Curiosity-flash hold on T4, Flashfreeze (with its exact red-only coverage), Day of Black Sun X=2 (what it kills on both sides), and Wasp on the Axe carrier sticking.
  • hold_or_fire rewritten: correct first windows (their T3 on the play, their T4 on the draw), Blade re-equip rather than first equip, Kaito-on-4 tradeoff, Curiosity-flash exception, never Tidebinder a Hex-able trigger, deploy as a body by T5-6 rather than rot.
  • counts_recommendation: cut 2 Wan Shi Tong (search trigger never fires; Erode's search is ours) instead of a land or Spell Snare; keep 23 lands; noted Flashfreeze and Day of Black Sun as further options over We Say Thee Nay!.
  • Verified our own cards' oracle text against data/opponents.json (Requiting Hex {B} MV<=2, Strategic Betrayal, Day of Black Sun, We Say Thee Nay!, Flashfreeze, Shoot the Sheriff non-outlaw, Kaito -2 and ninjutsu, Enduring Curiosity flash and return clause, Annul, Spell Snare, Spell Pierce, Wan Shi Tong).
  • Added Kíli-with-Hex-in-hand to dont_bother; added Boots ward to dont_bother.
vs. Orzhov Lifegain4% TB: goodWasp: good▼
Turn 1 gain fuel is Hinterland Sanctifier (4, "Whenever another creature you control enters, you gain 1 life") and Case of the Uneaten Feast (4, "Whenever a creature you control enters, you gain 1 life"); turn 2 payoffs are Amalia (4, "Whenever you gain life, Amalia explores"), Starscape Cleric (2, "Whenever you gain life, each opponent loses 1 life"), Essence Channeler / Leonardo (+1/+1 counter per gain), and Lunar Convocation (3, end-step drain); turn 3 adds Haliya (more fuel plus an end-step draw), Moseo (end-step Infusion reanimation) and Zoraline (recursion on enter/attack). Every creature they cast becomes 1-3 gain events, and each event fires every payoff, so one 1-drop can be 3 explores plus 3 drain. Their two lock pieces are STATIC and aimed at us: Voice of Victory (3, MV 2) "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" switches off our whole instant-speed game on the turn nearly all their triggers happen, and Clarion Conqueror (2, MV 3) "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated" switches off Kaito's loyalty abilities, Floodpits Drowner's shuffle, Map tokens and Ghost Vacuum (not ninjutsu, not Tidebinder/Wasp triggers). They kill by drain (Cleric, Convocation, sideboard Enduring Tenacity) and by out-grinding 1-for-1 removal with Moseo/Zoraline/Case/Emptiness recursion. Their removal: Erode (2 main, {W} instant, "Destroy target creature or planeswalker": kills Kaito on their turn, when he has no hexproof), Emptiness (2 main, sorcery-speed: evoked for {B}{B} it is a 2-mana "three -1/-1 counters" that ends any blank), Strategic Betrayal (1 main), and post-board Shoot the Sheriff (2, instant), Pyrrhic Strike (1, instant, kills Tidebinder MV 3 or Enduring Curiosity but not Wasp MV 2), Seam Rip (1, exiles Wasp/Drowner/Siren), Ultima (2, 5-mana wrath) and Ancient Vendetta (2, exiles up to four copies of a named card from our hand, graveyard and library).
Tishana's Tidebinder
Every payoff in the deck is a CREATURE with a lifegain trigger, so a countered trigger sticks as a full blank (Amalia loses ward and explore, Cleric loses the drain, Moseo loses Infusion, creature-form Tenacity loses drain AND return). Bonus: Tidebinder targets the ABILITY on the stack, never Amalia herself, so her Ward-Pay 3 life does not trigger against it. The catch is Voice of Victory: its lock is static (untouchable) and forbids casting Tidebinder on their turn, which is when ~90% of the counterable triggers happen. Rule of the matchup: Tidebinder is premium while Voice is not on the battlefield (or is Wasp-blanked); under an un-blanked Voice it is a sorcery-speed 3/2 with exactly one good line (counter Amalia's Ward trigger when you target her on your turn). Their answers that undo a blank: Erode ({W} instant), evoked Emptiness for {B}{B} (2 mana, sorcery speed, three -1/-1 counters), post-board Shoot the Sheriff and Pyrrhic Strike (instants). Kill the blanked payoff before their next untap when you can.
#1Amalia Benavides Aguirre
"Whenever you gain life, Amalia Benavides Aguirre explores. Then destroy all other creatures if its power is exactly 20."
WhyThe card-selection-plus-growth engine, and the only thing in the deck that taxes our targeted removal (Ward-Pay 3 life). Countering the FIRST explore of a turn blanks her before the second and third gain events resolve, so one Tidebinder eats a whole chain. Ward does not trigger: Tidebinder targets the trigger on the stack, not the permanent.
WhenTheir turn, after a creature spell resolves and the first Sanctifier/Case/Haliya gain resolves: Amalia's explore goes on the stack, Tidebinder in response. Only possible when Voice of Victory is absent or Wasp-blanked. On the play, their turn 3 (Haliya/Moseo enter with Sanctifier + Case gains) is the best window of the game and is live only if they skipped turn-2 Voice; on the draw you rarely have 3 open on their turn 3, so treat this as a turn-5+ window. Under an un-blanked Voice, see priority 3 (her Ward trigger on OUR turn).
AfterLoses ward, the explore trigger and the 20-power wrath while Tidebinder lives; keeps every +1/+1 counter already on her (a blanked Amalia is a tax-free target for Requiting Hex, Shoot the Sheriff, Kaito -2 or Drowner). Explores that already resolved stay resolved. Ward comes back the moment Tidebinder leaves.
RiskErode / evoked Emptiness ({B}{B}) / post-board Sheriff or Pyrrhic Strike on Tidebinder restores her. Do not wait for a 'perfect' multi-gain turn if Voice may land next turn: the window closes.
#2Starscape Cleric
"Whenever you gain life, each opponent loses 1 life." (Offspring ETB, "when this creature enters, create a 1/1 token copy of it", is a late-game bonus window.)
WhyThe drain is their real clock against us; each gain event is a point per Cleric. Counter its FIRST drain of a gain chain and the rest of the chain does nothing through it. If they ever pay the extra {2}{B} for offspring, counter the token-creation ETB instead: the token is denied, the mana stays spent, and Cleric is blank before any gain resolves.
WhenDrain: in response to its first "each opponent loses 1 life" trigger, their turn (Voice absent or blanked). Offspring: their main phase as Cleric resolves; the token ETB and the Sanctifier/Case gain triggers stack together, respond targeting the token trigger. Almost always Cleric is a naked turn-2 2/1 against a removal deck; plan for the drain window.
AfterLoses flying, the drain and "This creature can't block" (so a blanked Cleric CAN block). Keeps the 2/1 body. A token that already resolved is a separate permanent and keeps its drain.
RiskOnly 2 copies main; Requiting Hex ({B}) or Shoot the Sheriff kills it for less if you do not need the 3/2 body.
#3Amalia Benavides Aguirre (Ward trigger, on OUR turn)
"Ward, Pay 3 life." (Whenever Amalia becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls, counter it unless that player pays 3 life.)
WhyThe one Tidebinder line that works under an un-blanked Voice of Victory, because it happens on your turn. When you Requiting Hex, Floodpits Drowner (ETB or shuffle), Kaito -2 or Wasp her, the Ward trigger goes on the stack; Tidebinder counters it. Your spell/ability resolves with no 3-life tax, Amalia is the trigger's source so she is blanked (matters most with Drowner/Kaito -2, where she survives), and the blank carries through their whole next turn.
WhenYour main phase (or your end step for Drowner): target Amalia, let Ward trigger, flash Tidebinder targeting the Ward trigger before it resolves. Legal under Voice: Voice stops our spells only during THEIR turn.
AfterAmalia blanked (no ward, no explore) while Tidebinder lives; your targeting spell resolves untaxed. If Hex killed her the blank is moot but you saved 3 life and kept a 3/2.
RiskCosts 3 extra mana and a second card to save 3 life; do it when the blank carries value (Drowner stun-then-shuffle, Kaito -2) or when 3 life is the difference in the race.
#4Moseo, Vein's New Dean
"When Moseo enters, create a 1/1 black and green Pest creature token" and Infusion: "At the beginning of your end step, if you gained life this turn, return up to one target creature card with mana value X or less from your graveyard to the battlefield, where X is the amount of life you gained this turn."
WhyTheir recursion engine against our 1-for-1 removal. Countering the ETB denies the Pest and blanks Moseo, switching off every future Infusion while Tidebinder lives; countering Infusion itself saves the specific Amalia/Zoraline/Haliya they were about to rebuy. Shoot the Sheriff cannot kill Moseo (Warlock = outlaw) and Requiting Hex misses (MV 3); only Bitter Triumph, Kaito -2 or a blank handles him.
WhenETB: their main phase after Moseo resolves, Pest trigger on the stack. Infusion: beginning of their end step, trigger on the stack with its graveyard target chosen. Both need Voice absent/blanked.
AfterLoses flying and Infusion; keeps 2/1 body. A Pest already made keeps its own "Whenever this token attacks, you gain 1 life".
RiskPrefer the ETB when Tidebinder is likely to survive; prefer Infusion when the graveyard target is Amalia or Zoraline.
#5Enduring Tenacity (sideboard, creature form only)
"Whenever you gain life, target opponent loses that much life." and "When Enduring Tenacity dies, if it was a creature, return it to the battlefield under its owner's control. It's an enchantment."
WhyDoubles every gain into damage and normally survives removal. Countering its first drain WHILE IT IS STILL A CREATURE blanks it; a blanked Tenacity that dies has no death trigger, so Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph then remove it for good.
WhenIn response to its first "target opponent loses that much life" trigger (their turn unless the gain happened on ours), then kill it while Tidebinder is still on the battlefield. The window exists only in its first (creature) life.
AfterVanilla 4/3 while Tidebinder lives; no drain, no return. Once it has died and returned as a noncreature enchantment, Tidebinder still counters one drain but blanks nothing (ruling 3: source is not an artifact/creature/planeswalker) and Wasp cannot target it at all.
RiskPost-board only (1 copy). If you miss the blank, countering the return trigger keeps it dead once (source in graveyard, nothing blanked).
#6Emptiness
"When this creature enters, if {W}{W} was spent to cast it, return target creature card with mana value 3 or less from your graveyard to the battlefield." / "When this creature enters, if {B}{B} was spent to cast it, put three -1/-1 counters on up to one target creature." Evoke {W/B}{W/B}.
WhyModes depend on COLORS SPENT, not on how it was cast. Evoked for {B}{B} it is a 2-mana sorcery-speed kill on Tidebinder, Wasp, Drowner, Siren or a 4/3 Curiosity: countering that ETB saves your blank. Evoked for {W}{W} it is a 2-mana Amalia/Moseo/Haliya rebuy: countering it wastes the card. A 6-mana hardcast can pay {W}{W} and {B}{B} and trigger BOTH; Tidebinder stops one and blanks Emptiness, but the other trigger already on the stack still resolves (abilities on the stack exist independently of their source).
WhenAs Emptiness resolves its ETB trigger(s) go on the stack; if evoked, the evoke sacrifice trigger is there too. Target the reanimate or the -1/-1 trigger, never the sacrifice. Their main phase: Voice absent/blanked only.
AfterEvoked: Emptiness is blanked but the already-stacked sacrifice still removes it; they spent 2 mana for nothing. Hardcast: vanilla 3/5 while Tidebinder lives (one mode may still have resolved).
RiskKaito is not a creature on their turn, so the -1/-1 mode never hits him. Expect the {B}{B} evoke the turn after you land a blank; with two Tidebinders that is the second one's best job.
#7Zoraline, Cosmos Caller
"Whenever Zoraline enters or attacks, you may pay {W}{B} and 2 life. When you do, return target nonland permanent card with mana value 3 or less from your graveyard to the battlefield with a finality counter on it." and "Whenever a Bat you control attacks, you gain 1 life."
WhyRecursion plus a 3/3 flying vigilance blocker. Let the enter/attack trigger resolve and let them PAY {W}{B} and 2 life, then counter the reflexive "When you do" trigger: mana and life stay spent, nothing returns, Zoraline (the reflexive trigger's source) is blanked. On turns she does not pay, her Bat trigger (Cleric, Channeler, Convocation Bat tokens or Zoraline herself attacking) is also a blank window.
WhenAfter the enter/attack trigger resolves and they choose to pay: reflexive trigger on the stack targeting a graveyard card; Tidebinder then. Their main phase or declare-attackers, so Voice must be absent/blanked.
AfterLoses flying, vigilance, the Bat-gain and both recursion triggers; keeps 3/3. Anything already returned stays.
Risk1 copy; Bat Cleric (not an outlaw) so Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph also fine; Hex misses (MV 3).
#8Haliya, Guided by Light (hardcast)
"Whenever Haliya or another creature or artifact you control enters, you gain 1 life." and "At the beginning of your end step, draw a card if you've gained 3 or more life this turn."
WhyFuel plus card draw. Her own entry triggers the gain; countering it blanks her before she ever gains, removing a gain source and the end-step draw.
WhenHardcast Haliya ({2}{W}): her ETB gain trigger on the stack, their main phase (Voice absent/blanked). If she was WARPED for {W}, do not spend Tidebinder or Wasp on her: she is exiled at their end step on her own (see traps).
AfterVanilla 3/3 while Tidebinder lives; loses both triggers. Life already gained stays.
RiskRarely the best target if Amalia/Cleric are on the table; fuel is redundant (Sanctifier x4, Case x4), payoffs are not.
#9Hinterland Sanctifier
"Whenever another creature you control enters, you gain 1 life."
WhyWhen it is their ONLY gain source on the battlefield, blanking it turns off every payoff at once. Otherwise prefer the payoff.
WhenIn response to its gain trigger as their creature enters (their main phase, Voice absent/blanked). For the same effect on OUR turn under Voice, use Wasp instead (see Wasp list).
AfterVanilla 1/2. Other gain sources (Case, Haliya) keep working.
RiskLow body value; Requiting Hex kills it for {B}.
#10Case of the Uneaten Feast (Solved activation)
"Solved, Sacrifice this Case: Creature cards in your graveyard gain 'You may cast this card from your graveyard' until end of turn."
WhyTheir big rebuy turn. The Case is sacrificed as a cost, so countering the ability leaves them with no Case and no permission; a clean 1-for-1 that erases a whole turn plan.
WhenTheir main phase when they activate it, before it resolves. Requires Voice absent/blanked.
AfterCase is an enchantment: nothing loses abilities (it is gone anyway). Creatures already cast from the graveyard stay.
RiskOnly reachable without Voice; do not hold Tidebinder waiting for it.
#11Curious Farm Animals (sac ability)
"{2}, Sacrifice this creature: Destroy up to one target artifact or enchantment." (its "When this creature dies, you gain 3 life" is a separate trigger)
WhyTheir only main-deck answer to Enduring Curiosity (Enchantment Creature). Countering the destroy keeps the Cat; Farm Animals stays sacrificed.
WhenIn response to the activation. The death trigger (gain 3) triggered when they paid the cost and sits above the destroy; Tidebinder counters one or the other, not both. If they activate it on OUR turn (to kill a Curiosity mid-combat), Tidebinder is castable in response even with Voice out: Voice only stops our spells during their turn.
AfterNothing loses abilities (source is in the graveyard). Curiosity survives; they still gain 3 unless that was the trigger you countered.
RiskClarion Conqueror on their side forbids this activation entirely.
#12Essence Channeler / Leonardo, Cutting Edge
Channeler: "Whenever you gain life, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature." and "When this creature dies, put its counters on target creature you control." Leonardo: "Whenever you gain life, put a +1/+1 counter on Leonardo." (Lifelink is static.)
WhyBlanking Leonardo removes lifelink, a gain source we cannot otherwise switch off; blanking Channeler removes its conditional flying/vigilance and the death counter-transfer. Filler use.
WhenIn response to a growth trigger, or (Channeler) in response to the die trigger when you kill a big one on OUR turn: counters vanish instead of moving, and that response is legal under Voice because it is your turn.
AfterCounters already on them stay. Leonardo keeps 1/1 plus counters, no lifelink; Channeler keeps 2/1 plus counters.
RiskRemoval is cheaper: both die to Requiting Hex.
#13Seam Rip (sideboard)
"When this enchantment enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls with mana value 2 or less until this enchantment leaves the battlefield."
WhyIt targets Wasp, Drowner or Siren; countering the ETB saves the creature and leaves a blank enchantment.
WhenIn response to the ETB (their main phase, Voice absent/blanked).
AfterSeam Rip is an enchantment: nothing loses abilities; it just does nothing.
Risk1 copy, sideboard.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Flash in Tidebinder to counter the lock or the Mobilize trigger. "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" is a static ability and cannot be targeted. Mobilize 2 ("Whenever this creature attacks, create two tapped and attacking 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens") IS triggered, but it only triggers on their turn, when the static forbids casting Tidebinder in the first place. Answer Voice on the stack (Spell Snare, MV 2) or on your turn (Requiting Hex for {B}, Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph); Wasp is the backup.
Clarion Conqueror: Tidebinder it to turn Kaito/Drowner back on. It has no activated or triggered abilities at all (Flying and "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated" are static), so Tidebinder has nothing to target. Wasp blanks it; Shoot the Sheriff (Dragon, not an outlaw) or Bitter Triumph kills it; Hex misses (MV 3). Conqueror does NOT stop Tidebinder or Wasp, whose effects are triggered.
Emptiness (evoke): Counter the evoke sacrifice trigger. Evoke's "sacrifice it" is a triggered ability and can be countered, but doing so gifts them a 3/5 for 2 mana while their reanimate/-1/-1 ETB still resolves. Always target the ETB effect, never the sacrifice.
Haliya, Guided by Light (Warp): Counter the warp exile at their end step, or blank a warped Haliya to keep her from leaving. Warp's exile is (almost certainly) a delayed trigger created by the SPELL when they cast her for {W}, not an ability of the Haliya permanent: countering it keeps their 3/3 on the battlefield for one mana and blanks nothing (a one-shot Tidebinder), and conversely a Wasp- or Tidebinder-blanked warped Haliya is still exiled at end step (same as a dashed creature under Humility still returning). Under either reading the play is the same: never counter it, and never spend a blank on a warped Haliya.
Leonardo, Cutting Edge (Sneak): Counter Sneak as he comes in tapped and attacking. Sneak is an alternative cost for casting a spell and "enters tapped and attacking" is a replacement; neither is an ability on the stack. Spell Snare does counter the Leonardo spell (MV 2 regardless of Sneak), but Sneak happens during THEIR declare blockers step, so only when Voice is absent or Wasp-blanked. Tidebinder can only hit his growth trigger afterward.
Case of the Uneaten Feast / Lunar Convocation: Blank the enchantment engine. Both are enchantments; Tidebinder checks the source type on resolution and an enchantment never loses abilities. You only counter that one instance (1 life, one end-step drain, one Bat, one draw). The creatures cast from the graveyard after a Solved Case are spells being cast, not abilities: miss the activation and there is nothing to counter.
Amalia Benavides Aguirre (the 20-power wrath): Hold Tidebinder for the "destroy all other creatures if its power is exactly 20" moment. It is part of the explore trigger, so it is counterable, but this list never realistically reaches exactly 20 power. Spend Tidebinder on the ordinary first explore; the wrath is not what beats you.
Curious Farm Animals: Countering the sac ability also stops the 3 life; or kill Farm Animals on your turn to 'manufacture' a Tidebinder window under Voice. Sacrificing is a cost, so "When this creature dies, you gain 3 life" already triggered and is a separate object on the stack; one Tidebinder counters one of them. And killing it yourself fires their ENTIRE engine (Amalia explore, every Cleric drain, Channeler/Leonardo counters) off the 3 life while Tidebinder stops exactly one of those triggers: you spent removal on a 1/1 and gave a drain deck 3 life. Kill Farm Animals only when it is about to sacrifice itself at Enduring Curiosity.
Enduring Tenacity (returned form): Blank it after it has come back as an enchantment. Returned Tenacity is a noncreature enchantment: Tidebinder can still counter one drain but nothing is blanked (ruling 3), and Wasp cannot target it. The blank-then-kill line only exists while it is a creature.
Godless Shrine, Shattered Sanctum, Concealed Courtyard, Starting Town, Leyline of the Void: Counter the enters-tapped / pay-2-life / Leyline effects. "As this land enters, you may pay 2 life" and "enters tapped unless" are replacement effects; Leyline's "If a card would be put into an opponent's graveyard... exile it instead" is a replacement effect and its opening-hand clause is a static rule. Starting Town's "{T}, Pay 1 life: Add one mana of any color" is a mana ability. None can be targeted.
Pyrrhic Strike / Ultima / Ancient Vendetta / Erode (post-board and main removal spells): Tidebinder can protect a blank from their removal. These are spells, not abilities; Tidebinder never interacts with them. Only Spell Snare (Erode is MV 1, so no), We Say Thee Nay! or Spell Pierce do, and Voice makes all of those dead on their turn. Protect a blank by killing the blanked payoff before their untap, not by holding counters.
Not worth it
Pest token "Whenever this token attacks, you gain 1 life": one life on their turn; blanking a 1/1 token is not worth the card.Lunar Convocation: its "{1}{B}, Pay 2 life: Draw a card", its end-step drain and its second end-step trigger ("if you gained and lost life this turn, create a 1/1 black Bat creature token with flying") are all counterable, but the source is an enchantment (no blank) and each is a 1-for-1 at best; only if you were casting Tidebinder as a body anyway.Case of the Uneaten Feast "Whenever a creature you control enters, you gain 1 life" and its end-step solve trigger: one life / a delayed solve, no blank; hit the payoff the life would feed instead.Dalkovan Encampment "{2}{W}, {T}: Whenever you attack this turn, create two 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens": land, no blank, and it is their turn (Voice).Petrified Hamlet "When this land enters, choose a land card name": counterable and it leaves the Hamlet nameless, but it happens on their main phase; take it only when unlocked and nothing better is on the stack.Amalia's Ward trigger against your own WASP: legal and it blanks her, but that is 5 mana of flash to do what Tidebinder alone does against any Amalia trigger; the Ward line is for Hex/Drowner/Kaito -2 (priority 3).Essence Channeler's die trigger when it carries 1-2 counters: too small to spend the card on.Decorum Dissertation's Paradigm recast: sideboard 1-of that happens in their first main phase (Voice lock), and at most it denies one draw-two; skip.Voice of Victory's Mobilize tokens' "Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step": a delayed trigger; countering it keeps THEIR tokens alive. Never.
The Wondrous Wasp
Wasp is our only card that blanks a creature on OUR turn with the blank carrying through their whole turn, which is exactly what an un-blanked Voice of Victory forces us to do. First job: Clarion Conqueror (Requiting Hex cannot touch MV 3; blanking it turns Kaito's loyalty, Drowner's shuffle and Map tokens back on). Second job: pre-empt the gain chain on your main phase by blanking the payoff (Cleric, Amalia at the cost of 3 life for ward, Sanctifier when it is their only fuel), so their next turn's gains do nothing. Wasp-on-Voice is the BACKUP, not the default: Requiting Hex ({B}, MV 2), Shoot the Sheriff (Human Bard), Bitter Triumph and Spell Snare on the stack answer Voice permanently for 1 card, while a Wasp blank is undone by Erode ({W} instant) or a {B}{B} evoked Emptiness. Fragile 2/1: dies to Erode, Emptiness counters, Shoot the Sheriff, Seam Rip (MV 2), Day-of-Black-Sun-style sweepers; every blank ends the moment it leaves. No hexproof in their 75; the only ward is Amalia's Pay 3 life.
#1Clarion Conqueror
WhyBlanks "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated", re-enabling Kaito's +1/0/-2, Drowner's shuffle, Map explores and sideboard Ghost Vacuum, and it removes flying from and taps their best flying blocker. Hex misses it (MV 3), so Wasp or Sheriff/Bitter Triumph are the answers.
WhenYour main phase 1, before you want to activate Kaito or Drowner. Legal under Voice (your turn). Conqueror does not stop Wasp's ETB (it is triggered).
AfterVanilla tapped 3/3 while Wasp lives. Your activations work only while Wasp is on the battlefield; use them the same turn or kill Conqueror while it is blank.
#2Starscape Cleric
WhyBlank the drain, their clock, BEFORE their next gain chain; no ward, so it is free. The blank carries through their whole turn as long as Wasp survives.
WhenYour main phase (works under Voice) or their beginning of combat when unlocked. Tapping matters little (it cannot block anyway; a blanked Cleric loses "can't block" but Wasp has tapped it).
After2/1 with no flying and no drain while Wasp lives.
#3Amalia Benavides Aguirre
WhyBlank stops all explores for their turn and removes ward, so follow-up Hex / Shoot the Sheriff / Kaito -2 / Drowner are untaxed while Wasp lives. Tapping her before attackers stops a grown Amalia swinging.
WhenYour main phase (under Voice) for the pre-empt/ward-strip line, or their beginning of combat (pre-attackers) when unlocked. Wasp's trigger targets Amalia, so Ward triggers: pay 3 life or Wasp's trigger is countered (Wasp still enters as a 2/1 flier). With a Tidebinder in hand on your turn, counter the Ward trigger instead of paying (Tidebinder list, priority 3).
AfterLoses ward and explore; keeps all +1/+1 counters. Ward returns when Wasp leaves.
#4Voice of Victory
WhyBackup answer to the lock when you hold no Hex/Sheriff/Triumph or need them for Amalia: while Wasp lives you can cast spells on their turn again (Tidebinder, counters, flash Curiosity), Mobilize is gone, and the 1/3 blocker is tapped.
WhenYour main phase 1. Once Voice has resolved you cannot cast anything on their turn until it is blanked or dead.
AfterLoses the lock and Mobilize; keeps 1/3, stays tapped until their untap. The lock returns instantly if Wasp dies; while Erode is on the stack targeting Wasp the lock is still off, so if you drew removal by then, respond by killing Voice.
#5Hinterland Sanctifier
WhyWhen Sanctifier is their only gain source on the battlefield, a main-phase Wasp blank switches off every payoff for their whole next turn, and it is castable under Voice because it is your turn. Skip when Case or Haliya are also out.
WhenYour main phase, when it is the lone fuel piece.
AfterVanilla 1/2 while Wasp lives; Case/Haliya gains still work.
#6Enduring Tenacity (sideboard, creature form)
WhyTap-and-blank, then kill it with Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph: with no abilities when it dies, the "return it to the battlefield... It's an enchantment" trigger never happens. Permanent answer to a card built to beat removal.
WhenAny time you also have removal mana; on your turn under Voice is fine. Kill it before Wasp leaves, and only while it is still a creature (Wasp cannot target the returned enchantment form).
AfterVanilla 4/3 until dead; gone for good if killed while blank.
#7Zoraline, Cosmos Caller
WhyTapped before attackers she cannot attack, so no attack-trigger recursion and no "Whenever a Bat you control attacks, you gain 1 life"; the blank also strips flying/vigilance so she stops blocking Wasp/Siren/Beavers.
WhenTheir beginning of combat when unlocked; or your turn / your end step to pre-empt.
AfterVanilla 3/3 while Wasp lives.
#8Moseo, Vein's New Dean
WhyA blanked Moseo has no Infusion at their end step and no flying. Wasp on your turn carries the blank through their next end step (as long as Wasp survives), and Hex/Sheriff cannot kill him.
WhenYour turn, or their second main phase before the end step begins (unlocked only).
AfterVanilla 2/1; a Pest already made keeps its ability.
#9Curious Farm Animals
WhyA blanked Farm Animals has neither its "{2}, Sacrifice: Destroy up to one target artifact or enchantment" (their only main-deck answer to Enduring Curiosity) nor "When this creature dies, you gain 3 life", so a Requiting Hex on it then denies both the Cat kill and the 3-life engine restart. Mostly a post-board consideration (1 main, 3 after boarding).
WhenYour main phase with {B} still open for Hex; kill it while blank.
AfterDead with no life gained; if you do not kill it, it is a full Farm Animals again when Wasp leaves.
#10Leonardo, Cutting Edge / Haliya, Guided by Light (hardcast)
WhyLeonardo loses lifelink (a gain source). Haliya loses both gain and draw triggers and is a 3/3 blocker worth tapping for an attack. Never on a warped Haliya (she leaves anyway).
WhenYour main phase before attackers (blocker removal) or their turn pre-attack when unlocked.
AfterLeonardo keeps counters, no lifelink; Haliya vanilla 3/3.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Wasp is the default answer to Voice. Requiting Hex ({B}, MV 2) kills it permanently on your turn for 1 card and 1 mana; Shoot the Sheriff (Human Bard) and Bitter Triumph do too, and Spell Snare gets it on the stack. Wasp-on-Voice costs 2 mana, is undone by a {W} Erode, and spends the one card that can blank a payoff on your turn. Use Wasp on Voice only when you hold no removal or removal is earmarked for Amalia.
Voice of Victory: Kaito -2 (tap + two stun counters) on Voice lifts the lock. Stun and tap do nothing to a static ability; "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" keeps working. Only losing abilities (Wasp) or leaving the battlefield turns it off.
Voice of Victory: Once Wasp blanks Voice the lock is handled. The lock returns the instant Wasp leaves. Erode is a {W} instant and Shoot the Sheriff kills a Human Hero; expect them to kill Wasp in their upkeep or evoke Emptiness for {B}{B} on their main phase. Their removal spell is on the stack before the lock returns, so a kill spell for Voice cast in response keeps the lock off for good.
Any attacker: Flash Wasp after attackers are declared to stop the attack. Tapping does not remove a creature from combat. Resolve Wasp in their beginning of combat step (or your end step) to stop an attack; mid-combat it only blanks (e.g., Leonardo loses lifelink; Zoraline's attack trigger is already on the stack and still resolves).
Amalia Benavides Aguirre: Wasp is a free tap on Amalia. Wasp targets the creature, so Ward-Pay 3 life triggers; if you decline, Wasp's trigger is countered and you get a plain 2/1 flier. Tidebinder on her explore trigger (or on the Ward trigger itself) is the ward-free route.
Clarion Conqueror: You must Wasp the Conqueror before Tidebinder or Wasp can work. Conqueror stops activated abilities only; Tidebinder's and Wasp's effects are triggered abilities and work through it. It also does not stop Kaito's ninjutsu (an ability of a card in hand, not of a permanent). Wasp it when you need Kaito's loyalty abilities, Drowner's shuffle or Map tokens.
Case of the Uneaten Feast / Lunar Convocation / returned Enduring Tenacity: Blank the enchantments. Wasp targets creatures only; returned Tenacity is a noncreature enchantment.
Essence Channeler: Blanking shrinks it. It keeps its +1/+1 counters; it only loses the conditional flying/vigilance, the growth trigger and the death counter-transfer. Still an X/X body.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • Break the lock, then strike: your main phase, kill Voice of Victory with Requiting Hex / Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph (or Spell Snare it on the stack the turn before); if you have no removal, Wasp targeting Voice (tapped, blanked) instead. Attack with Kaito/Dream Beavers/Spyglass Siren, then on THEIR turn you are free to cast again: Tidebinder on Amalia's first explore, Cleric's first drain or Moseo's Infusion, Shoot the Sheriff on whatever matters, Enduring Curiosity at flash speed.
  • Lock insurance (Wasp-on-Voice games only): if they Erode or Shoot the Sheriff your Wasp during their turn, the lock is still off while that spell is on the stack; respond with Requiting Hex / Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph on Voice (Human Bard, MV 2, not an outlaw) so the lock never comes back.
  • Counter Voice on the stack: Spell Snare hits Voice, Amalia, Starscape Cleric (offspring does not change mana value), Leonardo (even cast via Sneak, but only when Voice is absent/blanked since Sneak happens on their turn), Essence Channeler and Lunar Convocation: 16 two-drops, which is why holding {U} on turn 1 on the draw is right even though Voice itself shows up by their turn 2 only about one game in three.
  • Ward strip under Voice: on your turn, target Amalia with Requiting Hex / Floodpits Drowner / Kaito -2 (or Wasp), let Ward trigger, Tidebinder counters the Ward trigger. Your spell or ability resolves untaxed and Amalia is blanked (no ward, no explore) through their next turn. This is the only Tidebinder line that works under an un-blanked Voice. With Drowner, the follow-up "{1}{U}, {T}: Shuffle this creature and target creature with a stun counter" is also untaxed while the blank lasts (needs Clarion Conqueror absent or Wasp-blanked).
  • Protect the blank: the turn after you blank Amalia/Cleric, expect Emptiness evoked for {B}{B} (three -1/-1 counters on Tidebinder or Wasp, 2 mana, their main phase). Tidebinder #2 in response to that ETB trigger saves the blank and the evoke sacrifice still removes Emptiness. Better still, kill the blanked payoff (now ward-free) before their untap.
  • Save the Cat: when they activate Curious Farm Animals' "{2}, Sacrifice this creature: Destroy up to one target artifact or enchantment" at Enduring Curiosity, Tidebinder counters the destroy (legal even under Voice if they do it on our turn); Farm Animals stays sacrificed and they still gain 3 (separate trigger). Pre-emptively, Wasp-blank Farm Animals and Hex it: no Cat kill, no 3 life.
  • Permanent Tenacity kill (post-board): while it is still a creature, Wasp or Tidebinder blank Enduring Tenacity, then Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph it; a blanked Glimmer has no death trigger and does not come back. Day of Black Sun with X at least 4 does the same by itself ("Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures.") but it also kills your own Tidebinder (MV 3), Kaito (a 3/4 creature on your turn, no targeting so hexproof is irrelevant) and Enduring Curiosity (blanked, so it stays dead); X=2 (4 mana) is the usual setting: it wipes Amalia (no ward, it does not target), Cleric, Voice, Leonardo, Channeler, Sanctifier, Farm Animals (no 3 life) and tokens while sparing Tidebinder, Kaito and Curiosity, at the cost of your own Siren/Beavers/Drowner/Wasp.
  • Kaito under their locks: ninjutsu still works (activated from hand, on your turn, not a spell); Conqueror stops only his loyalty abilities, so Wasp the Conqueror first if you need -2. Kaito -2 on Amalia costs 3 life (ward) unless she is blanked; Kaito -2 on Voice does NOT lift the lock. Erode kills Kaito on their turn (he has hexproof only during yours), so do not run him into an open {W} when you could hold Tidebinder instead.
  • Reanimation tax: let Zoraline's enter/attack trigger resolve, let them pay {W}{B} and 2 life, then Tidebinder the reflexive "When you do, return target nonland permanent card" trigger; payment stays spent, nothing returns, Zoraline blanked. Same idea for Moseo's Infusion at their end step (target already chosen, counter before it resolves).
  • Vren, the Relentless (sideboard): "If a creature an opponent controls would die, exile it instead" shuts off everything that keys off dying (Farm Animals' 3 life, Channeler's counter transfer, Tenacity's return) and starves Moseo/Zoraline/Case of every creature our removal kills; Amalia's explore still puts creature cards straight into their graveyard, so the rebuy engines are weakened, not dead. Strategic Betrayal (exile a creature and their graveyard) finishes that job.
Hold or fire
The value of Tidebinder here is decided by whether Voice of Victory is on the battlefield un-blanked. (1) Voice not down: FIRE Tidebinder on the first high-value trigger you see (Amalia's first explore of a chain, Cleric's first drain, Moseo ETB/Infusion, Zoraline's paid reflexive trigger, creature-form Tenacity's first drain). Do not hold for a better window; every turn Voice may land and close their turn to you, and a flash 3/2 that blanks a payoff now beats a hypothetical 2-for-1 later. (2) Voice down and un-blanked: Tidebinder is a 3/2 with one live line, countering Amalia's Ward trigger when you Hex/Drowner/Kaito -2 her on your turn. Otherwise spend the turn killing Voice (Hex/Sheriff/Triumph) or, with no removal, Wasp on Voice, which makes Tidebinder live again next turn. (3) Wasp: with Clarion Conqueror out, Wasp it on your main phase and use Kaito/Drowner the same turn. With Voice out and removal in hand, kill Voice and keep Wasp to pre-blank a payoff (Cleric, Sanctifier-as-lone-fuel, Amalia for 3 life) on your main phase so their next gain chain does nothing; with no removal, Wasp on Voice. Without lock pieces, hold Wasp for their beginning of combat (tap Zoraline/Amalia/Leonardo before attackers, blank Cleric before the chain) or for your own turn to remove a blocker for a Kaito ninjutsu attack; fire it as a plain 2/1 flier only when you need the clock. (4) Mana competition, on the play: T1 Siren, T2 Hex/Drowner as needed, T3 HOLD Tidebinder for their turn 3 (their best trigger turn: Haliya/Moseo plus Sanctifier and Case gains, Amalia explores) rather than ninjutsu-ing Kaito; Kaito dies to a {W} Erode on their turn and is switched off by Conqueror, while a T3 blank on Amalia/Cleric is worth more. If they played Voice on turn 2, turn 3 is Hex on Voice (or Wasp) and Tidebinder waits for their turn 4. T4 Enduring Curiosity at their end step; from T5 (7 lands for both) Tidebinder and Curiosity stop competing. On the draw: hold {U} on turn 1 for Spell Snare (16 two-drops, Amalia above all; Voice itself by their turn 2 only ~35%), develop on turns 2-4, and treat Tidebinder as a turn-5+ card after the lock is handled. Post-board: they have 2 Ultima ("Destroy all artifacts and creatures. End the turn.") and 2 Ancient Vendetta; do not pour Wasp plus Tidebinder plus Curiosity onto the board into 5 open mana on their turn, and never rely on counters (Voice makes them dead on their turn). Never spend Tidebinder on an enchantment or land ability while a creature payoff is on the battlefield with abilities left to trigger.
How many to run
Game 1 stock (1 Tidebinder main, 2 Wasp main) is right. Post-board go to 2 Tidebinder and keep 2 Wasp; do not go to 3. Reasoning: Tidebinder's targets are excellent (every payoff is a creature, ward-free Amalia blank, Moseo cannot be Hexed or Sheriff'd), but three Voice of Victory plus Erode/{B}{B} Emptiness/Sheriff/Pyrrhic Strike make the counter half dead or undone too often for a third copy, Ancient Vendetta (2 SB, exiles up to four of a named card from hand, graveyard and library) punishes stacking one 3-drop, and the sideboard has higher-impact swaps: 2 Day of Black Sun (cast at X=2 for 4 mana: kills Amalia with no ward, Cleric, Voice, Leonardo, Channeler, Sanctifier, Farm Animals with no 3 life, and tokens; it is symmetric, so it also kills your Siren/Beavers/Drowner/Wasp, and X>=3 kills your own Tidebinder, X>=4 Kaito and Curiosity; never sweep at X>=3 with a live blank), 1 Vren (exile-on-death removes the death-trigger half of their engine), 1 Strategic Betrayal. Wasp stays at 2: it is the only card besides removal that answers Clarion Conqueror on our turn and the only way to blank a payoff through a Voice turn, and it is a body. Suggested swap (opinion, not oracle): IN 2 Day of Black Sun, 1 Vren, 1 Tidebinder, 1 Strategic Betrayal; OUT 2 We Say Thee Nay! (Voice makes reactive spells dead on their turn and their key spells are 1-2 mana), 2 Wan Shi Tong (flash body hosed by Voice; its "Whenever an opponent searches their library" trigger never fires here: Erode makes US search and Ancient Vendetta searches OUR library, not theirs), 1 Dream Beavers (its 1-point drain is erased by their gain; Siren still enables ninjutsu). KEEP both Bitter Triumph (our only instant-speed unconditional kill for Moseo, Zoraline, Haliya, Conqueror, Emptiness 3/5 and Tenacity, all of which Hex misses) and both Restless Reef (a Voice-proof, Conqueror-proof threat; do not cut lands while adding 4-5 mana sorceries). Ghost Vacuum is a reasonable extra bring-in for a Beavers (1 mana, legal under Voice, exiles Moseo/Zoraline/Case rebuy targets on demand) with the caveat that Clarion Conqueror switches it off and Farm Animals can kill it. Flashfreeze stays out (no red or green spells); Spell Pierce/Annul stay out (Voice). On the play be greedier with Tidebinder (you are ahead of Voice); on the draw lean on Spell Snare and removal to break the lock first.
Review trail: confidence high, 6 disputed, 18 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Brief statement 'Ward taxes Wasp's trigger and Tidebinder's trigger': rejected for Tidebinder. Ward (CR 702.21b) triggers 'Whenever this permanent becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls'. Tidebinder's trigger targets an ability on the stack, never the permanent, so Amalia's Ward-Pay 3 life does not trigger against it. It does trigger against Wasp, Floodpits Drowner (ETB and shuffle), Kaito -2, Requiting Hex and Shoot the Sheriff, each of which targets Amalia; and that Ward trigger is itself a counterable triggered ability of Amalia (now Tidebinder priority 3).
  • Reviewer 1/3 'Wan Shi Tong never fires is unverified': now verified and kept. Wan Shi Tong, Librarian: 'Whenever an opponent searches their library, put a +1/+1 counter on Wan Shi Tong and draw a card.' Erode: 'Its controller may search their library' (the controller of the destroyed creature, i.e. us). Ancient Vendetta: 'Search target opponent's graveyard, hand, and library' (they search OUR library, not theirs). Nothing in their 75 has them search their own library; Amalia's explore reveals, it does not search.
  • Reviewer 1/3 'Day of Black Sun quote unverified': now verified verbatim against the mono-black deck file: 'Each creature with mana value X or less loses all abilities until end of turn. Destroy those creatures.' Quote kept; the symmetric-damage note from reviewer 2 was accepted.
  • Reviewer 1 on Haliya Warp: accepted as a hedge, not a settled ruling. Under the Dash/Blitz model (CR 702.109a / 702.152a: the alternative-cost static creates a delayed triggered ability as the spell resolves; CR 603.7e makes the spell its source) countering the exile blanks nothing and blanking Haliya does not stop the exile. I could not re-check the exact Warp rule (CR 702.180) here, so the page states 'almost certainly' and gives advice that is identical under either reading: never counter the exile, never spend a blank on a warped Haliya.
  • Reviewer 2 'Wasp-on-Voice is a priority inversion': accepted, with the caveat that Wasp on Voice remains correct when you hold no Hex/Sheriff/Triumph or when that removal is needed for Amalia; the 'Erode undoes it' argument applies equally to any Wasp blank, so the real reason is that Hex is a permanent 1-mana answer to a MV-2 non-outlaw.
  • Reviewer 2 'Ghost Vacuum dismissal overstated': partially accepted; Vacuum is listed as a reasonable extra bring-in with the Conqueror/Farm Animals caveat, but it is not in the primary 5-for-5 swap because Vren plus Strategic Betrayal already cover the graveyard and the deck's main problem is Voice, not recursion.
Changes after review
  • Removed Emptiness from every 'instant-speed removal' list (it is a sorcery-speed creature spell) and rewrote its entry around colors SPENT: evoke for {B}{B} is a 2-mana -1/-1 kill, evoke for {W}{W} a rebuy, a WW+BB hardcast triggers both and Tidebinder stops only one.
  • Added Pyrrhic Strike (SB instant, kills Tidebinder MV 3 / Curiosity, not Wasp MV 2), Ultima (SB wrath, 'End the turn') and Ancient Vendetta (SB, exiles 4 copies of a named card) to how_they_win, hold_or_fire and counts; added a trap that Tidebinder never interacts with these spells.
  • Corrected how_they_win: Curious Farm Animals is a death-trigger gain source (and their Curiosity answer), not turn-1 enter fuel; noted Erode destroys planeswalkers (kills Kaito on their turn) and that Conqueror does not stop ninjutsu or our triggers.
  • Reordered Wasp targets: Clarion Conqueror first, pre-emptive payoff blanks (Cleric, Amalia, Sanctifier) next, Voice of Victory demoted to a backup when no Hex/Sheriff/Triumph is available; added a trap explaining why Wasp is not the default Voice answer and made the 'lock insurance' line conditional.
  • Added Wasp targets Hinterland Sanctifier (lone-fuel blank on our turn under Voice) and Curious Farm Animals (blank then Hex: no Cat kill, no 3 life).
  • Added Tidebinder target 'Amalia Ward trigger on OUR turn' as the one under-Voice Tidebinder line; removed the 'manufacture a window by killing Farm Animals' combined line and turned it into a trap (it fires their whole engine for one counter).
  • Added Enduring Tenacity's returned noncreature form to target, trap and Wasp lists: Tidebinder counters one drain but blanks nothing, Wasp cannot target it; blank-then-kill only in creature form.
  • Rewrote the Haliya Warp trap as a hedge (delayed trigger created by the spell; blanking does not stop the exile; never counter it) and changed the Haliya target entry to 'never spend a blank on a warped Haliya'.
  • Starscape Cleric entry now leads with the first-drain window; offspring demoted to a late-game bonus.
  • Zoraline entry now notes her 'Whenever a Bat you control attacks' trigger (Cleric/Channeler/Bat tokens) as an additional blank window.
  • Fixed the Farm Animals timing wording: Tidebinder is castable in response on our turn because Voice only forbids our spells during THEIR turn (nothing of ours is being activated).
  • Leonardo Sneak trap now notes Spell Snare on a Sneaked Leonardo needs Voice absent/blanked (Sneak happens on their turn).
  • Vren line corrected: shuts off death-based pieces and starves rebuys of killed creatures, but Amalia's explore still fills the graveyard directly, so Moseo/Zoraline/Case are weakened, not dead.
  • Day of Black Sun: quote verified verbatim; added the symmetric self-damage (X=2 kills our Siren/Beavers/Drowner/Wasp; X>=3 our Tidebinder; X>=4 Kaito and Curiosity) and the X=2 default.
  • Sideboard plan fixed: keep both Bitter Triumph (only instant kill for Moseo) and both Restless Reef; cut 1 Dream Beavers instead; Ghost Vacuum reframed as a reasonable optional bring-in; Ancient Vendetta cited as a further reason to stop at 2 Tidebinder.
