WhyFour copies, the most frequent Tidebinder window in the deck, and it lands exactly on the turn we want our 4th mana for Kaito or Curiosity. 15 of our 23 lands are nonbasic; the replacement basic enters tapped with a stun counter, so our first untap step only removes the counter and it untaps at the second: we are a mana short on one full turn of ours and permanently down the utility land (Restless Reef, Soulstone Sanctuary, Hidden Lair). Countering keeps the land and turns a 4/5 flier into a 4/5 ground body our team can block.
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack, target already chosen. Voice-gated (needs no unblanked Voice). On the play we hold 3 lands on their turn 3, so a Voice-less opening lets this answer the turn-3 Hellkite; on the draw we have 2 lands then and the first realistic window is their turn 4. Cavern mana does not protect this trigger.
AfterLoses: flying and the ETB. Keeps: 4/5 body. If they cast it with Cavern of Souls our counterspells were never an option, so this is the correct answer, not a fallback.
RiskA 4/5 ground body still attacks into our 1/1s and 2/1s; if you hold both, Bitter Triumph or We Say Thee Nay! (non-Cavern) removes the body outright and is usually better.
#2Lorehold, the Historian
"At the beginning of each opponent's upkeep, you may discard a card. If you do, draw a card." (the miracle line is a static and is NOT the target)
WhyThe one high-value trigger in the deck that happens on OUR turn, so it works straight through Voice of Victory. Countering it stops the loot and any miracle {2} cast the loot draw would enable (Split Up on our untapped team, Desolation of Smaug, kicked Burst Lightning), and blanks a 5/5 flying haste into a 5/5 ground body. Tidebinder is the ONLY card that stops a loot already on the stack: Wasp blanking Lorehold in response does nothing to that trigger (it exists independently of its source once triggered).
WhenOur upkeep, immediately after the trigger goes on the stack (we are the active player and get priority first). All our untapped mana is available. Live regardless of Voice. Two copies at 5 mana, so it shows up in a minority of games; do not hold Tidebinder waiting for it.
AfterLoses: flying, haste, "Each instant and sorcery card in your hand has miracle {2}", the upkeep loot. Keeps: 5/5 body.
RiskThey can Burst Lightning / Erode / Charring Bite the Tidebinder on their turn and Lorehold returns to full; the loot that turn is still gone.
#3Smaug the Magnificent
"Whenever Smaug attacks, he deals damage equal to the number of Treasures you control to any target."
WhySmaug is the snowball engine: a 4/3 flying haste that grows its own damage every upkeep and can aim it at Kaito (loyalty loss on their turn), Curiosity or our face. Countering the attack trigger removes the damage AND leaves a wingless, hasteless 4/3 already committed to attacking: Enduring Curiosity (4/3) blocks and kills it (Curiosity returns as an enchantment unless Rest in Peace is out), Tidebinder (3/2) trades with it, or an animated Restless Reef (4/4 deathtouch, castable under Voice because it is an ability, not a spell) eats it. Ranked below Magmatic because Smaug is 3 copies and its first hasty attack usually carries only 0-2 Treasures; if Smaug survives to our turn, Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph on our turn is the cleaner answer.
WhenTheir declare attackers step, attack trigger on the stack with its target chosen. Voice-gated. Kaito can never block here: he is a creature only during OUR turn.
AfterLoses: flying, haste, the attack-damage trigger, the upkeep Treasure trigger. Keeps: 4/3 body, legendary status, Treasures already made (they still tap for mana). Ends the moment Tidebinder leaves.
RiskBurst Lightning kills Tidebinder at instant speed and Smaug gets everything back.
#4Dragonhawk, Fate's Tempest
"Whenever Dragonhawk enters or attacks, exile the top X cards of your library, where X is the number of creatures you control with power 4 or greater. You may play those cards until your next end step. At the beginning of your next end step, Dragonhawk deals 2 damage to each opponent for each of those cards that are still exiled."
WhyOne trigger is X free cards plus 2 damage per unplayed card (X counts Dragonhawk itself, so at least 1, typically 3-4). Countering the enters/attacks trigger means nothing is exiled and the end-step damage is never created; Dragonhawk is left a 5/5 ground body.
WhenTheir main phase with the ETB on the stack, or their declare attackers step with the attack trigger on the stack. Voice-gated. Backup: the delayed trigger at the beginning of THEIR next end step is itself a triggered ability and can be countered; if Dragonhawk already died (Bitter Triumph), the damage still happens by last-known information, and Tidebinder on that delayed trigger is the only way to stop it (nothing loses abilities if Dragonhawk is gone).
AfterLoses: flying and the enters/attacks trigger. Keeps: 5/5 body. Cards already exiled by a previous resolved trigger stay playable and an already-created end-step damage trigger still fires unless countered separately.
RiskSingle copy in the main deck; never hold for it.
#5Maelstrom of the Spirit Dragon
"{4}, {T}, Sacrifice this land: Search your library for a Dragon card, reveal it, put it into your hand, then shuffle."
WhyA non-mana activated ability with no target, so it is legal. Costs stay paid: the land is already sacrificed, the {4} is spent, and any Treasures cracked to pay it are gone too ("{T}, Sacrifice this artifact" is the cost). They get no Dragon. That is a turn of mana plus a land for 3 of ours, and because they usually crack it at OUR end step it is castable through Voice.
WhenAny time the ability is on the stack; most often our end step. If they crack it on their own turn under Voice we cannot respond.
AfterNothing loses abilities (source is a land and is already in the graveyard). Trade-off: countering the search means no search happens, so Wan Shi Tong's "Whenever an opponent searches their library" trigger (counter + draw) never fires; still counter it, a denied Dragon beats one card.
RiskA careful pilot will not crack it into 3 open blue mana; the value is mostly in forcing bad sequencing.
#6Sheltered by Ghosts (sideboard)
"When this Aura enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls until this Aura leaves the battlefield."
WhyTheir post-board answer to Kaito, Enduring Curiosity or Wan Shi Tong. Countering the ETB saves our permanent.
WhenTheir main phase, Aura ETB on the stack. Voice-gated. Ward {2} from the Aura does not tax Tidebinder: ward triggers when the PERMANENT becomes a target, and Tidebinder targets the ability.
AfterNothing loses abilities (source is an enchantment). The Aura stays and keeps granting +1/+0, lifelink and ward {2}. Cheaper alternative if the exile already resolved: kill the enchanted creature (Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph, paying ward {2}); the Aura leaves and our permanent returns.
RiskAnnul on the Aura spell is cleaner when we are allowed to cast it (their turn, so Voice must be gone).
#7Monica Rambeau
Back face: "Whenever you cast a noncreature spell, put a +1/+1 counter on each other creature you control." Front face: "{2}{R}{W}{W}: Transform Monica Rambeau. Activate only as a sorcery."
WhyThe transformed face is hexproof, so Wasp cannot touch her, but Tidebinder targets the ABILITY: countering the cast trigger strips flying, hexproof, prowess and the trigger while Tidebinder lives. Countering the front-face transform wastes their whole turn of mana and blanks her (flying, prowess, transform).
WhenBack-face trigger: whenever they cast a noncreature spell, INCLUDING on our turn (a Burst Lightning or Erode at our blocker or our Wasp puts the trigger on the stack during our turn, so this works through Voice). Transform: their main phase only, Voice-gated. Their own Clarion Conqueror stops them activating the transform at all.
AfterLoses: flying, hexproof, prowess, counter trigger (back) or flying, prowess, transform (front). Keeps: printed body and counters already placed on other creatures.
RiskSingle copy; opportunistic only.
#8Nova Hellkite (ETB ping, to save a lock piece)
"When this creature enters, it deals 1 damage to target creature an opponent controls."
WhyThe ping kills exactly our X/1s: Wasp (2/1), Floodpits Drowner (2/1), Spyglass Siren, Dream Beavers. Counter it only when the target is a Wasp holding Voice/Clarion blank or a Drowner holding a stunned Dragon. Bonus: Nova loses flying and haste for the turn.
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack. Voice-gated, but if the Wasp being pinged is the one blanking Voice we CAN respond: Voice has no static while Wasp lives, and a spell already on the stack resolves even if Wasp then dies.
AfterLoses: flying, haste, the ETB text. Keeps: 4/5 body. IMPORTANT: a warped Nova is STILL exiled at the beginning of the next end step. Warp's exile clause is a delayed triggered ability created while the spell was on the stack, not an ability the permanent carries (same as dash and blitz), so 'loses all abilities' does not stop it; they may recast it from exile later at full cost.
RiskOnly take this trade to save a lock piece.
#9Seam Rip (sideboard)
"When this enchantment enters, exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls with mana value 2 or less until this enchantment leaves the battlefield."
WhyIts only legal targets in our deck are Wasp, Drowner, Siren, Beavers, Map tokens; worth countering only when the target is a Wasp holding Voice or Clarion blank, or a Drowner locking a Dragon.
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack. Voice-gated (unless Wasp has Voice blanked, which is exactly when this matters).
AfterNothing loses abilities (source is an enchantment). Seam Rip stays as a blank enchantment.
RiskSpending 3 mana to protect a 2-mana creature is only right when that creature is the lock piece.
#10Ward {2} on a creature wearing Sheltered by Ghosts (sideboard)
Granted ward {2}: "Whenever this permanent becomes the target of a spell or ability an opponent controls, counter it unless that player pays {2}."
WhyWard is a triggered ability of the ENCHANTED CREATURE. When our Bitter Triumph, Shoot the Sheriff, Kaito -2 or Wasp targets that creature, the ward trigger goes on the stack and Tidebinder can counter it: no {2} tax, our spell resolves, and because the source is a creature it loses ALL abilities while Tidebinder lives, including the Aura-granted lifelink and ward (the Aura's grant predates Tidebinder's effect, so ruling 2 does not save them). Fires on OUR turn when our removal targets it, so it works through Voice.
WhenOur turn, ward trigger on the stack, in response to our own targeted spell or ability.
AfterCreature loses everything including granted abilities; the Aura itself stays attached. If our removal then kills the creature the blank is moot and the Aura leaves, returning whatever it exiled.
Risk3 mana to dodge a {2} tax only makes sense because you also get a 3/2 body; usually just pay the 2.
#11Miracle trigger from Lorehold (any instant/sorcery in their hand)
Miracle {2}: "When you draw this card, if it's the first card you drew this turn, you may reveal it. When you reveal it this way, you may cast it by paying {2}..."
WhyIf we missed the loot window and they reveal Split Up or Desolation of Smaug in our upkeep, countering the miracle trigger stops that cast. Strictly worse than countering the loot itself.
WhenOur upkeep, after the loot resolves and they reveal. Works through Voice (our turn).
AfterNothing loses abilities: the source is a card in their hand. The card stays in hand and can be cast normally later.
RiskA real counterspell on the miracle-cast spell (legal, it is our turn) does the same job for less.
#12Sarkhan, Dragon Ascendant
"When Sarkhan enters, you may behold a Dragon. If you do, create a Treasure token."
WhyDenying the Treasure delays their T3 Hellkite a turn, but the T2 Sarkhan is uncounterable on mana (we have at most 2 lands on their turn 2) and later copies matter less. Requiting Hex (MV 2) kills him for 1 on our turn.
WhenTheir main phase, ETB on the stack. Voice-gated.
AfterLoses: both triggers. Keeps: 2/2 body and counters already placed. Treasures already made keep working.
RiskAlmost never right after turn 3; listed for completeness.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Tidebinder in response to Voice to shut off the lock. "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" is a static ability. Tidebinder counters only activated and triggered abilities. Worse, once Voice is on the battlefield you cannot cast Tidebinder (a spell) during their turn at all. Handle Voice on your own turn: Requiting Hex (MV 2 target, 1 mana, permanent), Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph, Wasp, or Spell Snare the Voice spell itself while no other unblanked Voice is out.
Voice of Victory (Mobilize 2): Counter the mobilize trigger when Voice attacks. "Whenever this creature attacks, create two tapped and attacking 1/1 red Warrior creature tokens" is a real triggered ability, but a Voice that attacks with mobilize is by definition an unblanked Voice on the battlefield, and its static forbids us casting anything on their turn. Never counterable in practice.
Clarion Conqueror: Tidebinder Clarion to turn Kaito's loyalty abilities back on. "Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated" is static; Clarion has no activated or triggered ability to target. Wasp (loses all abilities), Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph fix it; Requiting Hex cannot (MV 3), and Kaito's -2 cannot because Clarion stops it. Clarion does not stop Tidebinder's or Wasp's ETBs (triggered), Kaito's ninjutsu (an ability of a card in hand; Clarion's wording refers to permanents on the battlefield, CR 109.2), or our land abilities (Restless Reef, Soulstone Sanctuary).
Nova Hellkite (warp): Counter the warp cast, the recast from exile, or expect a blanked warped Nova to stay on the battlefield. Casting for the warp cost is an alternative cost and casting from exile is casting; neither is an ability. And the end-step exile is a delayed triggered ability created while the spell was on the stack (CR 702, warp; same template as dash and blitz), not an ability of the permanent, so a Nova blanked by Tidebinder or Wasp is STILL exiled at the next end step. The only counterable pieces are the ETB ping and the delayed exile trigger itself.
Momo, Friendly Flier: Counter the cost reduction so Nova cannot be warped for 2. "The first non-Lemur creature spell with flying you cast during each of your turns costs {1} less to cast" is static. Requiting Hex (Momo is MV 1) or Wasp handles Momo; Tidebinder cannot.
Lorehold, the Historian (miracle line): Counter "Each instant and sorcery card in your hand has miracle {2}". Static; it grants an ability to cards in hand. The counterable things are the loot trigger in our upkeep (priority 2) or the miracle trigger of the revealed card, whose source is a card in hand, so Lorehold loses nothing when you counter that.
Treasure tokens, Maelstrom's mana, Cavern of Souls, Sunbillow Verge: Counter the mana to stop the Dragon. All mana abilities; Tidebinder cannot target them (reminder text on the card). Cavern's "that spell can't be countered" rides on a mana ability and protects the spell only; the Dragon's ETB trigger is fair game.
Burst Lightning, Erode, Charring Bite (Twinmaw omen), Desolation of Smaug, Split Up, Dawn's Truce: Tidebinder the burn or the sweeper. Spells, not abilities. Kicker is an additional cost paid while casting. Desolation's "Add four mana" is part of a spell's effect. Use Spell Snare (Charring Bite and Dawn's Truce are MV 2) or a hard counter, and only during a turn no unblanked Voice is out if it is their turn.
Sheltered by Ghosts / Seam Rip / Rest in Peace: Counter the ETB and the enchantment is neutralized. The ETB is countered (good), but the source is an enchantment, so nothing loses abilities: Sheltered keeps granting +1/+0, lifelink and ward {2}, and Rest in Peace's replacement effect ("If a card or token would be put into a graveyard from anywhere, exile it instead") never uses the stack and can never be countered. That replacement stops Enduring Curiosity from ever dying and returning.
Multiversal Passage, Sacred Foundry, Cavern of Souls, Inspiring Vantage: Counter the 'as this enters' choice or the tapped clause. "As this land enters" clauses are replacement effects and "enters tapped unless" is static. Nothing goes on the stack.
Doorkeeper Thrull (sideboard): Tidebinder the Thrull, or cast Tidebinder into 2 open mana with W after they show Thrull. "Artifacts and creatures entering don't cause abilities to trigger" is static and switches off Tidebinder's and Wasp's own ETBs (plus Drowner, Siren, Beavers, Wan Shi Tong). Thrull has flash: with 2 open mana they respond to a Tidebinder cast and it enters with no trigger. It is symmetrical, though: while Thrull lives THEIR Nova ping, Magmatic land destruction, Dragonhawk's enters half, Sarkhan's ETB and Twinmaw's lifegain do not trigger either, so the Tidebinder menu shrinks to Smaug's attack/upkeep triggers, Dragonhawk's attack half and end-step damage, Lorehold's loot, miracle and Maelstrom. Kill Thrull (Requiting Hex, MV 2, on our turn; Shoot the Sheriff; Bitter Triumph) before deploying either card.
Voice of Victory tokens: Counter "Sacrifice them at the beginning of the next end step". The window does not exist: that delayed trigger fires at THEIR end step on a turn an unblanked Voice attacked, so we cannot cast Tidebinder then (Voice's static). Even if Voice had died in combat and the window opened, countering it just lets THEM keep two 1/1s.
Not worth it
Momo's "Whenever another creature you control with flying enters, Momo gets +1/+1 until end of turn": one temporary point of power.Twinmaw Stormbrood's "When this creature enters, you gain 5 life": not worth 3 mana and a card; Wasp grounds the 5/4 for 2 if that is the goal.Sarkhan's Dragon-enters counter trigger: a 2/2 growing by one.Nova Hellkite's warp exile delayed trigger (their end step, Voice gone): countering it keeps their Nova on the battlefield permanently instead of exile-and-recast; that is neutral to bad for us. Whether Nova would then lose abilities is genuinely unsettled (CR 603.7d names 'that spell' as the source of a spell-created delayed trigger), so do not build a line on it.Monica's front-face prowess: +1/+1 until end of turn.Rest in Peace's ETB (exile all graveyards): our deck barely uses the graveyard, and the replacement effect that actually hurts Curiosity cannot be countered.Smaug's upkeep Treasure trigger: Smaug has haste, so this window only exists after he has already hit us once; if he survived to our turn, Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph on our turn (works under Voice) is the play, not a 3-mana blank that Burst Lightning undoes. Use it only when you have no removal and Voice is gone.
The Wondrous Wasp
Wasp is the better of our two cards here because it strips STATIC abilities, which Tidebinder cannot: it blanks Clarion Conqueror (turns Kaito, Drowner and Map tokens back on) and Voice of Victory (unlocks our whole instant-speed deck), and every one of their creatures except Voice, Sarkhan and Thrull has flying to lose, so a Wasp resolved at their beginning of combat skips an attack and grounds a Dragon for our blockers. Two catches: Voice also stops us casting Wasp on their turn, so the Voice blank is a sorcery-speed play on our turn, and the body is a 2/1 that dies to Burst Lightning, Erode, Nova Hellkite's 1-damage ping and Split Up, ending the effect at once. Because Requiting Hex (4), Shoot the Sheriff (2), Bitter Triumph (2) and Spell Snare (2) all answer Voice permanently, Wasp's best target is Clarion Conqueror, which only Shoot/Triumph can otherwise touch. Use the window it buys rather than counting on it lasting.
#1Clarion Conqueror
Why"Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated" turns Kaito into a hexproof 3/4 that cannot use loyalty abilities, stops Floodpits Drowner's shuffle and our Map tokens (and their own Treasures and Monica's transform). Requiting Hex cannot hit it (MV 3) and Kaito's -2 is switched off by it, so only Wasp, Shoot the Sheriff and Bitter Triumph answer it, and it is usually cast with Cavern mana so no counterspell does. Wasp blanks it and Kaito comes back online the same turn; it also loses flying, so a 3/3 ground body instead of a flying blocker for Wasp, Siren and Beavers.
WhenOur main phase 1, before activating Kaito or Drowner. Also fine on their turn if no unblanked Voice is out (their beginning of combat, before attackers, so it does not attack either).
AfterLoses the activation lock and flying; keeps 3/3. Kaito's loyalty abilities, Drowner's ability and Maps work while Wasp lives.
#2Voice of Victory
Why"Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn" is the whole reason our deck stumbles here. Wasp removes it (and mobilize), so on their next turn Tidebinder, counterspells, Bitter Triumph and Spell Snare work again; Voice becomes a 1/3 with nothing. Rank order by hand: with Requiting Hex (1 mana, permanent), Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph available, use THAT on Voice and keep Wasp for Clarion or a Dragon; Wasp goes to Voice only when no removal is in hand or the removal is needed for a Dragon.
WhenOUR turn only while Voice has abilities: main phase 1 so the tap also removes a blocker, or our end step. Note Wasp on Voice spends 2 of our 3 mana on turn 3, so the unlock usually pays off a turn later than removal would.
AfterVoice loses the spell lock and mobilize; keeps 1/3. A second Voice still locks us and needs its own answer. If Wasp dies, the lock returns immediately, but spells we already put on the stack still resolve.
#3Smaug the Magnificent
WhyBlanked Smaug has no flying, no haste, no Treasure and no attack damage: a 4/3 that Enduring Curiosity (4/3) kills in a block, Tidebinder (3/2) trades with, or an animated Restless Reef (4/4 deathtouch) eats. Kaito cannot block: he is a creature only during OUR turn. Tapping Smaug before attackers skips a whole attack.
WhenTheir beginning of combat step, BEFORE attackers are declared (needs no unblanked Voice). Tapping an already-attacking Smaug does not remove it from combat. On our turn: tap it so it cannot block, and it makes no Treasure next upkeep while Wasp lives.
AfterLoses flying, haste, both triggers; keeps 4/3 and its existing Treasures.
#4Lorehold, the Historian
WhyBlank removes flying, haste, the miracle grant and FUTURE loots. It does NOT stop a loot already on the stack: once triggered in our upkeep the ability exists independently of Lorehold, so only Tidebinder counters that one. Wasp is the play when you want a permanent-ish ground-and-silence for 2 mana on your main phase; Tidebinder is the play for the loot in progress.
WhenOur main phase (works through Voice), or their beginning of combat if Voice is gone. The tap keeps a 5/5 from blocking Curiosity or Kaito on our turn.
AfterLoses flying, haste, miracle grant, upkeep loot on later upkeeps; keeps 5/5. This upkeep's discard/draw (and any miracle from it) still happens if it was already on the stack.
#5Dragonhawk, Fate's Tempest
WhyThe attack trigger is the repeatable value; a Wasp resolved before attackers means no attack trigger and no flying.
WhenTheir beginning of combat before attackers (Voice gone), or our turn. The ETB has usually already resolved; cards already exiled stay playable and an already-created end-step damage trigger still fires.
AfterLoses flying and the enters/attacks trigger; keeps 5/5.
#6Magmatic Hellkite / Nova Hellkite / Twinmaw Stormbrood (after their ETB)
WhyOnce the ETB has resolved they are 4/5, 4/5 and 5/4 fliers. Wasp grounds one so our ground blockers hold it and our fliers (Wasp, Siren, Beavers) get through for Enduring Curiosity draws; the tap skips one attack. Twinmaw's 5 life is already gained; only the body matters.
WhenTheir beginning of combat before attackers (Voice gone) to stop an attack; our turn to remove a flying blocker.
AfterLoses flying (and haste on Nova); keeps the body. A warped Nova is STILL exiled at the beginning of the next end step (the exile is a delayed trigger created on the stack, not an ability of the permanent), so Wasp on a warped Nova buys exactly one skipped attack; the effect ends when Nova leaves.
#7Momo, Friendly Flier
WhyBlanks the static cost reducer so Nova cannot be warped for 2 and their fliers cost full price. Requiting Hex (Momo is MV 1) does this permanently for 1 mana; use Wasp only when Hex is not in hand.
WhenOur turn 2 if we have nothing better; only worth it when it visibly delays a Dragon a turn.
AfterLoses flying and both abilities; keeps 1/1.
#8Monica Rambeau (front face only)
WhyNo hexproof until transformed, so Wasp for 2 mana blanks flying, prowess and the {2}{R}{W}{W} transform; she never becomes the hexproof back face while Wasp lives.
WhenOur turn, or their turn if Voice is gone. Must happen before she transforms.
AfterLoses flying, prowess, transform; keeps 3/3 (printed).
#9Sarkhan, Dragon Ascendant
WhyStops future Treasures and counters; a 2/2 with nothing. Requiting Hex is the better answer.
WhenOur turn, only if no better body is on their side.
AfterLoses both triggers; keeps 2/2 and counters already on it.
Traps
Voice of Victory: Flash Wasp at their beginning of combat to tap Voice and stop mobilize. While Voice has abilities we cannot cast Wasp during their turn. The Voice blank is a sorcery-speed play on our turn, full stop.
Voice of Victory (Kaito -2 / Drowner as the answer): Kaito -2 or Floodpits Drowner's stun handles Voice. Tap plus stun counters do nothing to a static ability; Voice keeps locking us while tapped. Only losing all abilities (Wasp) or removal ends it.
Any attacking creature: Wasp an attacker to remove it from combat. Tapping an attacking creature does not remove it from combat; it still deals damage. Smaug's already-triggered attack damage is not stopped by Wasp either; that is a Tidebinder job.
Monica Rambeau (transformed): Wasp the transformed Monica. Back face has hexproof; Wasp's trigger cannot target her. Tidebinder can still counter her cast trigger because it targets the ability, not the creature.
Creature wearing Sheltered by Ghosts: Wasp it for 2 mana. Ward {2}: pay 2 more or the trigger is countered. Ward does not tax Tidebinder (it targets an ability, and ward triggers when the permanent becomes a target); Tidebinder can even counter the ward trigger itself.
Dawn's Truce (sideboard): Wasp during a turn they cast Dawn's Truce. "You and permanents you control gain hexproof until end of turn": Wasp's trigger has no legal target. Tidebinder is unaffected (it targets abilities).
Doorkeeper Thrull (sideboard): Wasp with Thrull on the battlefield or into 2 open mana with W. "Artifacts and creatures entering don't cause abilities to trigger": Wasp enters, nothing happens, and it cannot even blank the Thrull. Remove Thrull first (Requiting Hex, Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph).
Second Voice of Victory / second Wasp: One Wasp fixes the lock, or two Wasps hold two things. Each Voice's static is independent; with two Voices out, blanking one changes nothing until the other is answered. And Wasp is legendary: casting a second while the first is out makes the legend rule (CR 704.5j) put one in the graveyard before the new trigger resolves, so the old blank ends and you can only ever hold ONE creature blanked. The second copy is a rebuy that moves the blank, not a second lock.
Warped Nova Hellkite: Wasp it to keep it on the battlefield as a permanent vanilla 4/5. The warp exile is a delayed triggered ability created while the spell was on the stack; 'loses all abilities' does not remove it. Nova is exiled at the next end step regardless and comes back later with everything.
Lines with the rest of the deck
  • OPENING (Momo T1): Requiting Hex kills Momo (MV 1) for {B}. On the play, cast it at their end step T1 (we have 1 untapped land); on the draw, cast it in our T1 main phase. Without Momo the T2 Nova costs {2}{R} and they have 2 lands, so the turn-2 warped Hellkite never happens. On the play with no Hex: we have 2 lands on their turn 2, so either We Say Thee Nay! on the warped Nova is a hard counter (both their lands are tapped, they cannot pay {2}; unless the warp was paid with Cavern mana), or Wasp at their beginning of combat taps it before it attacks (Nova's ETB ping already happened, so Wasp is safe from it that turn). On the draw with no Hex, block or race it.
  • VOICE ANSWER ORDER: Requiting Hex (MV 2, 1 mana, permanent) > Shoot the Sheriff / Bitter Triumph > Spell Snare on the Voice spell (only legal while no OTHER unblanked Voice is on the battlefield: always for the first copy, later copies only after the earlier one is dead or blanked) > Wasp. All on OUR turn except Snare. Hex also kills Sarkhan and Doorkeeper Thrull; it cannot touch Clarion (MV 3).
  • UNLOCK LINE: our main phase, Wasp targets Voice (tap + blank). Their turn: Tidebinder is live for Magmatic's ETB, Smaug's attack trigger or Dragonhawk; Bitter Triumph, Spell Snare, We Say Thee Nay! work again. If they Burst Lightning or ping the Wasp, respond while Wasp is still alive: Voice has no static until Wasp actually dies, and anything already on the stack resolves.
  • VOICE STOPS SPELLS, NOT ABILITIES: under an unblanked Voice on their turn we can still animate Restless Reef ({2}{U}{B}: 4/4 deathtouch blocker that kills any Dragon) or Soulstone Sanctuary, use Floodpits Drowner's shuffle on a stunned Dragon, and crack Map tokens on our turn. Reef is also Clarion-proof (a land, not an artifact/creature/planeswalker, when you activate it).
  • KAITO RESCUE: Wasp on Clarion Conqueror in main phase 1, then Kaito -2 on a Hellkite (tap + two stun counters) the same turn, or Drowner's {1}{U},{T} shuffle on the stunned Dragon. Clarion never stopped ninjutsu (card in hand) or our flash ETBs (triggered), only activations. WARNING: Kaito's ninjutsu returns an unblocked attacker to hand; if that attacker is the Wasp holding Clarion or Voice blanked, the blank ends at once. Ninjutsu off Siren or Beavers instead, or recast Wasp the same turn for a fresh tap + blank.
  • THE TURN-4 CHOICE: if they have a Dragon on the battlefield, or Momo / a Sarkhan Treasure pointing at a 4-power flier next turn, do NOT tap out for Kaito on an open board (a 4/5 flying haste attacks him to 0-1 loyalty). Hold Tidebinder + Hex, or flash Enduring Curiosity at their end step; cast Kaito only when he can -2 the lone Dragon the same turn (Clarion not out) or their board has no 4-power flier. Ninjutsu Kaito in off an unblocked flier for {1}{U}{B} leaves a mana up.
  • UPKEEP TIDEBINDER: with Lorehold on their side keep 3 mana in mind for our own upkeep: Tidebinder on the loot trigger works through Voice, kills the miracle-Split-Up risk, and leaves a 3/2 that blocks a 1/3 Voice and kills it. Wasp cannot stop a loot already on the stack.
  • SMAUG BLOCK: Tidebinder on Smaug's attack trigger, then block the wingless 4/3 with Tidebinder (trade), Enduring Curiosity (kills it; if Curiosity dies it returns as an enchantment unless Rest in Peace is out), or an animated Restless Reef (4/4 deathtouch, activatable under Voice). Kaito never blocks: he is a creature only during our turn.
  • CURIOSITY DRAWS: every blank removes flying, so Wasp (2/1 flying), Siren and Beavers connect past grounded Dragons; each hit is a card with Enduring Curiosity on the battlefield.
  • CAVERN GAP: a Dragon cast with Cavern of Souls mana cannot be countered (this includes Clarion Conqueror, a Dragon), but its ETB can. When they lead with Cavern, hold Tidebinder rather than a counterspell for the Hellkite, once Voice is handled; hold Wasp/Shoot/Triumph for Clarion.
  • SHELTERED BY GHOSTS (post-board): if the exile already resolved, killing the enchanted creature (Shoot the Sheriff or Bitter Triumph, paying ward {2}) makes the Aura leave and returns our Kaito/Curiosity; usually cheaper than having held Tidebinder for the ETB. Tidebinder can counter the ward trigger itself to skip the {2} and blank the creature.
  • DRAGONHAWK CLEANUP: if we killed Dragonhawk after its trigger resolved, the end-step damage still happens; Tidebinder on that delayed trigger at their end step (Voice gone) stops it, though nothing loses abilities.
  • DO NOT DEPLOY INTO THRULL (post-board): if they show 2 open mana with W and Doorkeeper Thrull is plausible, cast Tidebinder or Wasp only when the trigger does not matter, or bait Thrull with the less important one first; Hex/Shoot/Triumph the Thrull on our turn, then deploy. Remember Thrull also blanks THEIR Hellkite ETBs and Sarkhan's Treasure while it lives.
Hold or fire
While an unblanked Voice of Victory is on the battlefield: kill Voice on our next main phase with Requiting Hex / Shoot / Triumph if we have one, otherwise Wasp it; Tidebinder fires only at Lorehold's loot in our upkeep, at a Maelstrom activation on our turn, at Monica's back-face trigger if they cast an instant on our turn, or as a 3/2 body at our end step when we need a blocker. Once Voice is gone or blanked: hold Wasp for Clarion Conqueror whenever Kaito is in hand or on the battlefield, otherwise for Smaug or Dragonhawk at their beginning of combat before attackers, otherwise to ground the biggest Dragon before it attacks; hold Tidebinder for Magmatic Hellkite's ETB (4 copies, main-phase window, saves a land), then Smaug's attack trigger, then Dragonhawk's enters/attacks trigger, and fire at Nova's ping or Seam Rip's ETB only when the target is a lock piece (Wasp on Voice/Clarion, Drowner on a stunned Dragon). Before turn 4, prefer removing the body (We Say Thee Nay! on a non-Cavern Hellkite, Bitter Triumph) over blanking it when both are possible; a blanked 4/5 still attacks. Never hold Tidebinder past turn 6 waiting for a trigger; a 3/2 flash blocker that kills Voice and Warrior tokens is fine value. On the play we have 3 lands on their turn 3, so a Voice-less opening lets Tidebinder answer the turn-3 Hellkite ETB; on the draw we have only 2 lands then, so Wasp is our only turn-3 flash play and Tidebinder starts at their turn 4. Turn-4 rule: with a Dragon or a Momo/Treasure-enabled Dragon threatening, hold Tidebinder + Hex or flash Curiosity rather than tapping out for Kaito; deploy Kaito when he can -2 the only Dragon at once or via ninjutsu off an unblocked flier that is not the Wasp holding a blank.
How many to run
Keep stock: 1 Tidebinder main, 2 Wasp main, and do NOT bring the 2 sideboard Tidebinders in. Reasons, in order: (a) countering a Hellkite's ETB still leaves a 4/5 body that attacks into our 1/1s and 2/1s, while We Say Thee Nay!, Bitter Triumph and Shoot the Sheriff remove the body; (b) the blank dies to Burst Lightning (3), Erode and Charring Bite (2), all 1-2 mana; (c) Voice of Victory, present in roughly half of their openers (about 51% chance of none in the first 9 cards with 4 copies), removes the their-turn windows Tidebinder wants, and Clarion Conqueror is a static it cannot touch; (d) Cavern of Souls is only 3 of 26 lands, so our real counterspells are rarely blanked. The one copy earns its slot for Magmatic's ETB, Lorehold's loot and a flash 3/2 body. Wasp: both stay in. There is no legal cap from being legendary (CR 100.2a allows four; the legend rule only limits copies on the battlefield to one), so a 3rd Wasp from outside the stock 75 is legal and defensible here because it blanks the two statics that beat us, but the second copy on the battlefield is only a rebuy, never a second lock. Post-board slots should go to cheap sorcery-speed creature removal that works on our turn (Shoot the Sheriff, Bitter Triumph; Requiting Hex is already 4) rather than more reactive counters: Flashfreeze, Spell Pierce and Annul (their enchantments are cast on THEIR turn, so Annul is Voice-gated too) all need Voice gone. If their list shows the 2 Doorkeeper Thrull, value every flash ETB a notch lower and lean on removal further.
Review trail: confidence high, 3 disputed, 21 changes
Disputed reviewer points (kept as written)
  • Reviewer 2: downgrade Wasp verdict from 'good' to 'situational'. Rejected. Oracle: Voice of Victory and Clarion Conqueror are statics ('Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn'; 'Activated abilities of artifacts, creatures, and planeswalkers can't be activated'), and Wasp is the only card in our 75 that removes a static ('It loses all abilities'); Clarion is MV 3 (outside Requiting Hex) and is usually Cavern-cast (uncounterable), leaving only Shoot/Triumph/Wasp as answers. Every Dragon in their list has flying (Nova, Magmatic, Smaug, Lorehold, Dragonhawk, Twinmaw, Clarion), so the tap-before-attackers plus grounding is live in every game, not only Voice games. The reprioritization (Clarion over Voice, Voice only when no Hex/Shoot/Triumph) was accepted; the fragility caveats are in the summary.
  • Reviewer 1: countering the warp exile delayed trigger with Tidebinder makes Nova lose all abilities because 'the trigger's source is Nova'. Not accepted as stated. CR 603.7d: a delayed triggered ability created by a spell has that spell as its source; whether that counts as 'an ability of a creature' for Tidebinder once the spell has become a permanent is not settled by oracle text or the rulings provided. Reviewer 2 flagged the same uncertainty. Kept as an explicit hedge in dont_bother with no line built on it (the outcome, keeping their Nova permanently, is not desirable anyway).
  • Reviewer 2: 'drop the Smaug upkeep window'. Partially rejected. Oracle: Smaug has haste, so the window exists only after a first attack (accepted, moved out of the target entry), but it remains a legal Voice-gated Tidebinder target that blanks a 4/3 flying haste when we hold no Shoot/Triumph; kept in dont_bother as a fallback with that condition rather than deleted.
Changes after review
  • WARP (major, all 3 reviewers): reversed the claim. A warped Nova blanked by Tidebinder or Wasp is STILL exiled at the next end step; warp's exile is a delayed triggered ability created on the stack (dash/blitz template), not an ability of the permanent. Fixed Tidebinder target 8, Wasp Hellkite entry, traps, dont_bother, and removed the old changes_made bullet asserting the opposite.
  • KAITO BLOCKS (major): removed every claim that Kaito blocks Smaug; he is a creature only during our turn. Smaug blockers are now Curiosity, Tidebinder, Restless Reef.
  • WASP CAP (major): removed 'capped at 2 (legendary)'; stated the four-copy rule (CR 100.2a) and legend rule (CR 704.5j) correctly, and added the legend-rule consequence that two Wasps cannot hold two blanks.
  • SPELL SNARE 'always legal' (major): corrected to 'only while no other unblanked Voice is on the battlefield'.
  • NOVA PING vs TIDEBINDER (major/minor): 1 damage does not kill a 3/2; rewrote how_they_win with the asymmetric removal map (Nova ping kills Wasp only; Charring Bite kills Tidebinder/Drowner/Curiosity but cannot target flying Wasp and, as a sorcery, never Kaito).
  • MOMO-NOVA 'never answerable' (major): replaced with the Requiting Hex T1 line (both play and draw), We Say Thee Nay! on the tapped-out warp on the play, and Wasp at their beginning of combat on the play.
  • REQUITING HEX (missed, major): added as the primary sorcery-speed Voice/Momo/Sarkhan/Thrull answer throughout; Wasp on Voice demoted to fallback; Wasp priority 1 is now Clarion Conqueror.
  • TIDEBINDER PRIORITIES: Magmatic Hellkite ETB moved to 1 (4 copies, main-phase window, hits our mana on the Kaito turn); Lorehold 2; Smaug attack trigger 3; Smaug upkeep window moved to dont_bother as a no-removal fallback.
  • TURN-4 RULE (major): added explicit hold-vs-Kaito guidance to combined_lines and hold_or_fire.
  • MAELSTROM Treasures (minor): 'they keep the Treasures used to pay' corrected to 'Treasures cracked to pay are gone (costs stay paid)'; added the Wan Shi Tong search-trigger trade-off.
  • MONICA back face (minor): counter trigger is counterable on OUR turn when they cast an instant, so not strictly Voice-gated; only the transform is.
  • VOICE TOKENS trap (minor): rewritten; the sacrifice window never exists under an attacking unblanked Voice.
  • WASP vs LOREHOLD LOOT (minor): Wasp cannot stop a loot already on the stack; only Tidebinder can. Fixed Wasp target 4 and the UPKEEP line.
  • MAGMATIC stun math (minor): corrected to one full turn of ours down that mana (first untap removes the counter, second untaps).
  • ANNUL (minor): marked Voice-gated (their enchantments are cast on their turn).
  • DOORKEEPER THRULL symmetry (minor): added that Thrull also blanks their Hellkite ETBs, Sarkhan ETB, Twinmaw lifegain, Momo pump, shrinking our Tidebinder menu.
  • CLARION scope (minor): added that Clarion does not stop land abilities (Restless Reef, Soulstone Sanctuary) or ninjutsu; Reef is the Clarion-proof and Voice-proof deathtouch blocker.
  • KAITO NINJUTSU returning Wasp (missed): added warning that ninjutsu off the Wasp ends its blank; use Siren/Beavers or recast Wasp.
  • NEW TARGETS added: Sheltered by Ghosts' granted ward {2} as a Tidebinder target (source is the enchanted creature, works on our turn through Voice); Monica front face and Twinmaw Stormbrood as Wasp targets; Sheltered by Ghosts 'kill the enchanted creature to return the exiled permanent' line.
